```json
{
  "cmd": "node <<'NODE'\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst path = require('path');\nconst { execSync } = require('child_process');\n\nconst repo = '/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh';\nconst sharedRoot = path.join(repo, 'src/shared');\nconst sharedDirs = fs.readdirSync(sharedRoot, { withFileTypes: true })\n  .filter((d) => d.isDirectory())\n  .map((d) => d.name)\n  .sort();\n\nconst filesRaw = execSync(\n  \"rg --files src/tools -g '*.ts' -g '*.tsx' -g '*.mts' -g '*.cts'\",\n  { cwd: repo, encoding: 'utf8' }\n).trim();\n\nconst files = filesRaw\n  ? filesRaw.split('\\n').filter((f) => !/(^|\\/)__tests__(\\/|$)|\\.test\\.|\\.spec\\.|(^|\\/)__mocks__(\\/|$)/.test(f))\n  : [];\n\nconst importRe = /(?:import|export)\\s+[^'\"`]*?from\\s*['\"]([^'\"`]+)['\"]|import\\(\\s*['\"]([^'\"`]+)['\"]\\s*\\)/g;\n\nconst usage = new Map();\nfor (const d of sharedDirs) usage.set(d, new Set());\n\nfor (const rel of files) {\n  const abs = path.join(repo, rel);\n  const src = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');\n  const tool = rel.split('/')[2];\n  let m;\n  while ((m = importRe.exec(src))) {\n    const spec = m[1] || m[2] || '';\n    let sharedSubdir = null;\n\n    if (spec.startsWith('@/shared/')) {\n      sharedSubdir = spec.slice('@/shared/'.length).split('/')[0];\n    } else if (spec.startsWith('shared/')) {\n      sharedSubdir = spec.slice('shared/'.length).split('/')[0];\n    } else if (spec.includes('/shared/')) {\n      const idx = spec.indexOf('/shared/');\n      const rest = spec.slice(idx + '/shared/'.length);\n      sharedSubdir = rest.split('/')[0];\n    }\n\n    if (sharedSubdir && usage.has(sharedSubdir)) {\n      usage.get(sharedSubdir).add(tool);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nconst rows = sharedDirs.map((dir) => {\n  const tools = Array.from(usage.get(dir)).sort();\n  const count = tools.length;\n  const classification = count >= 3 ? 'true-shared' : 'feature-specific';\n  return { dir, count, classification, tools };\n});\n\nconst componentsByTool = new Map();\nconst componentImporterFiles = [];\nfor (const rel of files) {\n  const abs = path.join(repo, rel);\n  const src = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');\n  const tool = rel.split('/')[2];\n  let m;\n  while ((m = importRe.exec(src))) {\n    const spec = m[1] || m[2] || '';\n    let rest = null;\n    if (spec.startsWith('@/shared/components/')) {\n      rest = spec.slice('@/shared/components/'.length);\n    } else if (spec.includes('/shared/components/')) {\n      const idx = spec.indexOf('/shared/components/');\n      rest = spec.slice(idx + '/shared/components/'.length);\n    }\n    if (!rest) continue;\n    const top = rest.split('/')[0];\n    if (!top) continue;\n    if (!componentsByTool.has(top)) componentsByTool.set(top, new Set());\n    componentsByTool.get(top).add(tool);\n    componentImporterFiles.push({ component: top, tool, file: rel, spec });\n  }\n}\n\nconst keyComponents = ['MediaLightbox', 'ImageGenerationForm'];\nconst keySummary = {};\nfor (const k of keyComponents) {\n  const tools = Array.from(componentsByTool.get(k) || []).sort();\n  keySummary[k] = { toolCount: tools.length, tools };\n}\n\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({ rows, keySummary }, null, 2));\nNODE",
```

```json
}
```

```json
{
  "cmd": "python - <<'PY'\nimport os,re,glob\ncands=[]\nfor path in glob.glob('src/**/*.ts',recursive=True)+glob.glob('src/**/*.tsx',recursive=True):\n    if path.endswith('.test.ts') or path.endswith('.test.tsx') or path.endswith('.spec.ts') or path.endswith('.spec.tsx'):\n        continue\n    bn=os.path.basename(path)\n    if bn=='index.ts' or bn=='index.tsx':\n        continue\n    with open(path,'r',encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        txt=f.read()\n    if 'export' not in txt or ' from ' not in txt:\n        continue\n    # strip block comments and line comments for rough check\n    stripped=[]\n    in_block=False\n    for line in txt.splitlines():\n        s=line.strip()\n        if in_block:\n            if '*/' in s:\n                in_block=False\n                s=s.split('*/',1)[1].strip()\n            else:\n                continue\n        if s.startswith('/*'):\n            if '*/' in s:\n                s=s.split('*/',1)[1].strip()\n            else:\n                in_block=True\n                continue\n        if s.startswith('//') or not s:\n            continue\n        stripped.append(s)\n    if not stripped:\n        continue\n    def allowed(line):\n        return bool(re.match(r'^(export\\s+\\*\\s+from\\s+[\"\\\"].+[\"\\\"];?|export\\s*\\{[^}]*\\}\\s*from\\s+[\"\\\"].+[\"\\\"];?|export\\s+type\\s*\\{[^}]*\\}\\s*from\\s+[\"\\\"][^\"\\\"]+[\"\\\"];?)$',line))\n    if all(allowed(line) for line in stripped):\n        cands.append(path)\nfor p in sorted(cands):\n    print(p)\nPY"
}
```

select });\n  supabaseClientFactoryMock.mockReturnValue({ from });\n  return { eqProject, eqShot, from, order, select };\n}\n\nfunction mockChildQuery(response: { data: unknown; error: unknown }) {\n  const orderByCreatedAt = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(response);\n  const orderByChildOrder = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ order: orderByCreatedAt });\n  const eqParent = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ order: orderByChildOrder });\n  const select = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ eq: eqParent });\n  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ select });\n  supabaseClientFactoryMock.mockReturnValue({ from });\n  return { eqParent, from, orderByChildOrder, orderByCreatedAt, select };\n}\n\nfunction mockLiveTimelineQuery(response: { data: unknown; error: unknown }) {\n  const order = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(response);\n  const gte = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ order });\n  const eqShot = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ gte });\n  const select = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ eq: eqShot });\n  const from = vi.fn().mockReturnValue({ select });\n  supabaseClientFactoryMock.mockReturnValue({ from });\n  return { eqShot, from, gte, order, select };\n}\n\ndescribe('segmentOutputsQueries', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.clearAllMocks();\n  });\n\n  it('builds stable query keys for parents/children/live timeline', () => {\n    expect(buildParentGenerationsQueryKey('shot-1', 'project-1')).toEqual(\n      segmentQueryKeys.parents('shot-1', 'project-1'),\n    );\n    expect(buildParentGenerationsQueryKey('shot-1', null)).toEqual(\n      segmentQueryKeys.parents('shot-1', undefined),\n    );\n    expect(buildChildrenQueryKey('parent-1')).toEqual(segmentQueryKeys.children('parent-1'));\n    expect(buildLiveTimelineQueryKey('shot-1')).toEqual(segmentQueryKeys.liveTimeline('shot-1'));\n  });\n\n  it('fetchParentGenerations maps raw rows through transformToGenerationRow', async () => {\n    const raw = [{ id: 'p1' }, { id: 'p2' }];\n    const spies = mockParentQuery({ data: raw, error: null });\n\n    const result = await fetchParentGenerations('shot-1', 'project-1');\n\n    expect(spies.from).toHaveBeenCalledWith('shot_final_videos');\n    expect(spies.select).toHaveBeenCalledWith('*');\n    expect(spies.eqShot).toHaveBeenCalledWith('shot_id', 'shot-1');\n    expect(spies.eqProject).toHaveBeenCalledWith('project_id', 'project-1');\n    expect(spies.order).toHaveBeenCalledWith('created_at', { ascending: false });\n    expect(transformToGenerationRowMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);\n    expect(result).toEqual([{ id: 'mapped-p1' }, { id: 'mapped-p2' }]);\n  });\n\n  it('fetchParentGenerations normalizes and rethrows });\n});\nEOF",
